([GuillaumeLessard/qector-claude-plugin](https://github.com/GuillaumeLessard/qector-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/qector-space-time) · ★1)

- **qector-two-stage-css**: Two-stage CSS decoding for QECTOR (manual chapter 12). Independent X and Z decoders assume P(X, Z) = P(X) P(Z); depolarising noise violates this. Load for any question about depolarising noise, CSS sector coupling, feedforward X-&gt;Z syndrome updates, or the Theorem 13 joint-faithfulness guarantee.
